% —	Mahāmudrātilakatantra-nāma-mahāyoginītantrarājādhipati (Dpal phyag rgya chen po'i thig le zhes bya ba rnal 'byor ma chen mo'i rgyud kyi rgyal po'i mnga' bdag). Tôh. no. 420. Dergé Kanjur, vol. NGA, folios 66r.1-90v.7. Tr. by Shes rab gsang ba, Kam Chos kyi ye shes and Phyug mtshams Dgra bcom.

%\vparal{\vmaa\ 63.15}
%|me tog gser ni zhes su bstan| |spos ni shar du la ru brjod| |dri ni ma la ti ru bshad| |me mar sha ko na ki ra dran|

%|mig ni utpal shes par bya| |so ni a da na ru bstan| |mchu ni pa la wa ru shes| |mgrin pa gling bur bstan pa'o|

%|phung po nga ba zhes su bstan| |lag ngar myu gur bstan pa'o| |lag ba mtsho skyes bstan par grags| |nu ma dpal 'bras brjod pa'o|

%| dbus ni ri 'byung shes par bya| |lta ba ma da na ru bstan| |brla ni ka ta ma pa ru grags| |padma ja ga na ru dra na|

nakhā varāṅga kieśa[25\verso]rā nāsikā madakarṇikā |
%|byin pa dza na pi ru brjod| |rkang pa a tsa shi ra ra dran| |sen mo yan lag ge sar rab| |sna ni ma da sha ba ri|
śayanaṃ śātakasuma śaktaḥ patra iti smṛtaḥ |
aśakto nalinīlīnaṃ pakṣmagopakṣakaṃ smṛtam |
%|stan ni sha ta ku sum ste| |nus pa bu ni zhes su dran| |mi nus pa ni padma'i sdong | |thams cad ming gis rdza mar dran|
vṛddhaṃ sāgaram ity uktaṃ svādaṃ malam iti smṛtam |
duḥsvādaṃ cākṣaraṃ proktaṃ maunaṃ mṛgam ihocyate |
%|gzhon nu rdzing zhes bya bar grags| |'bar ma mtsho zhes bya bar dran|
%|rgan po rgya mtsho zhes par dran| |myong ba mi smra zhes su 'gyur|
%|myong ba ma yin gsal bar dran| |mi smra ri dgas zhes su brjod|

raso lāsyarasāvaśya rūpaḥ parvataśeṣaraḥ |
māraṇaṃ dhvanitaṃ proktaṃ jīva parāyataḥ smṛtaḥ |
%|ro ni sgeg po zhes su bstan| |gzugs ni re zhes brjod pa'o| |gsod pa sgra ni zhes su brjod| |tshe ni phug ron dran pa'o|
śrīmaṃ tamālakusumaṃ pataraṃ kalam ucyate |
taṃgaraḥ kaṃpitaḥ prāṇaḥ prāṇaḥ śunya iti smṛtaḥ |
%|grong ni tA la'i me tog ste| |grong khyer 'bras bu yin par brjod| |ljongs zhi 'dod pa zhes byar bstan| 'dod pa srog ces bya bar dran|

dinaṃ kumāraḥ kathitaḥ kanyā rātriḥ prakīrtitā |

%|nyin mo gzhon nur shes par bya| |mtshan mo bu mor rab tu grags| |mtshams ni "77a" dud med shes par bya| |nyin phyed bran du bstan pa'o| |'dus pa yi ni dbus gnas su| |rnal 'byor ma ni rtag tu brjod|

ataḥ paraṃ melāpakasthāne sarvayoginīnām aṣṭa[=aṅga]mudrālakṣaṇam ādiśyate |
% de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba| |yan lag phyag rgya sogs mtshan nyid|
ya ekāṅgulaṃ darśanyataṃ aṅgaṃ | yoṇīsva[w/tilde]rasya syāṅkanāthaṃ svāgataṃ karoti |
so pi tasyāṅgulidvayadarśanāt ta susvāgata – – yete |
%|gang zhig sor mo gcig ni ston| |bsnyun rmed pa zhes sbyan 'dren no| |sor mo gnyis ni bstan pa yis| |legs par bsnyun lan ljon pa yin|
yo madhyamā darśati tasyā anāmā darśanīyā | yā anāmāṃ darśayeta tasyā – – na – – – lā darśayitavyā |
%|gang zhig gung mo ston byed pa| |de la srin lag bstan par bya| |gang zhig srin lag ston byed pa| |de la lce ni bstan par bya|
yā paṭṭi<|kā|>mudrāṃ darśayet tasyās triśūlaṃ darśayet | yā śikhāṃ darśayet tasyā ṅgupradhānaṃ darśayet |
%|gang zhig gzhibs bston byed pa| |de la rtse gsum bstan par bya| |gang zhig gtsug phud ston byed pa| |de la mtshog ma bstan par bya|
yā lalāṭaṃ darśayati tasyā navagaṇḍaṃ prakaṭīkartavyam | yā trikaṭikāṃ darśayet tasyā cāntaṃ pradarśayet |
%|gang zhig dpral ba ston byed pa| |de la mkhur tshos bstan par bya| |gang zhig ltag khung ston byad pa| |de la sa gzhi bstan par bya|
yā hastau darśayet tasyāḥ kucau darśayitavyau | yā grīvāṃ darśayati tasyā bjano[\ms26\verso]daraṃ darśayet |
%|gang zhig lag gnyis ston byed pa| |de la lag ngar bstan par bya| |gang zhig mgrin pa ston byed pa| |de la lto ba bstan par bya|
yā coṣṭ[h]au spṛśati tasyā vasyacibukaṃ darśanīyā | yā daśanāṃ darśayet tasyāpy adharaṃ darśayitavyam |
%|gang zhig ma mchu ston byed pa| |de la kos ko bstan par bya| |gang zhig so ni ston byed pa| | de la lci ni bstan par bya|
[om.?] yā bhṛkuṭīṃ darśayati tasyāpi sīmantaṃ darśanīyam | yā mukhaṃ darśayati tasyānuvṛtyā pṛṣṭaṃ darśayitavyam ||   ||
% |gang zhig nu ma ston byed pa| |de la kos khur bstan par bya| |gang zhig lto ba ston byed pa| |de la lto ba bstan par bya| |gang zhig gsang ba ston byed pa| |de la ling ga bstan par bya| |gang zhig brla ni ston byed pa| |de la gzhang ni bstan par bya| |gang zhig rkang pa ston byed pa| |de la rkang mthil bstan par bya| |gang zhig sor mo ston byed pa| |de la sen mo bstan par bya| |gang zhig gtsang po ston byed pa| |de la rgya mtsho bstan par bya| |gang zhig nam mkha' ston byed pa| |de la nyi ma bstan par bya| |gang zhigs ni ston byed pa| |de la nam mkha' bstan par bya| |gang zhig khro gnyer ston byed pa| |de la mtshams ni bstan par bya| |gang zhig so ni ston byed pa| |de la mchu ni bstan par bya| |gang zhig mgrin pa ston byed pa| |de la kha ni bstan par bya| |gang zhig dpral ba ston byed pa| |de la rgyab ni bstan par bya| | "77b" 'di ni thams cad rnal 'byor ma'i| |yan lag rgya sogs mtshan nyid do|
iti śrīmahāmudrātilake sarvayoginīnāṃ mudrāp[r]atimudrāsaṃketavidhipaṭalaś caturdaśaḥ ||   ||
%|dpal phyag rgya chen po'i thig le las rnal 'byor ma thams cad kyi yan lag gi phyag rgya la sogs pa zhes bya ba'i le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o|| ||
athānyaṃ sampravakṣyāmi gauryādī[nāṃ] tu lakṣaṇam | yāgārākanakavarṇābhā ṣaṅkam(?!)ottamavigrahā |
%de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba| |gzugs dang rtags sogs mtshan nyid ni| |dmar zhing ser ba'i bud med gang | |padma'i dri ni rnam par gtong |
prakṛtyā saumyanayanā sānurāgām āvalokinī | nakhe raktotpalābhākhā netropāntāruṇapatrā
%|lta stangs rang bzhin zhi ba dang | |zhen pas lta ba'i rjes 'gro ba| |sen mo dmar po padma 'dra| |mig ni nyi ma'i 'od dang ldan|
kipālamadhye nalinaṃ pūjayantī surādinā | padmanarteśvarakulādi jñeyā sābjanāyikā ||
%|de la khyim na padma yod| |rtag tu mchod pa'i cho ga byed| |padma gar gyi dbang phyug rigs| |padma'i gtso mor de shes bya|
yā r-indīvaravarṇābhāti śūlāṅkitapaṭṭikā | gṛhe 'rcayantī kuliśaṃ padmasyopa[\ms27\recto]ri kiṃśukaiḥ |
%|smin mtshams par na rtse gsum yod| |lus ni sngo bsangs skya ba dang | |de yi khyim na rdo rje yod| |tang ku dmar pos mchod par bya|
śrīherukakulodbhūtā vijñeyā sā'mbikākṣilā (!? = akhilā?) |
% śrī-... vijñeyā highlighted in red
%|he ru ka dpal rigs 'byung ba'i| |kun gyi ma mor shes par bya|
yā raktā rakāmbujavarṇābhā caraṇābhyāṃ samujjvalā | bhrūmadhye tu triśūlena śaṃkho vā parilāñchitam |
%|rtse gsum gang gi dpral ba 'am| |yang na shag ti gsal 'gyur ba| |mig dmar gdod ni dmar pa dang | |de bzhin rkang pa lag pa dmar|
pārāvatiḥ [=pārāvataiḥ poss. pc.] kukkuraiś ca sārameyair <su>nityaśaḥ | krīḍate bālavat tatra vīrabhāryeti viśrutā |
gṛhe canakaṃ vaścakākhyaṃ (?) cihnam arcayate sadā | śrīmadvajrakulodbhūtā ḍākinī sā na saṃśayaḥ |
%|rtag tu phug ron la rtsed rtse| |de bzhin khyi dang khyim bya'o| |de yi khyim du rdo rje'i mtshan| |rtag tu gang zhig mchod byed pa| |dpal ldan rdo rje'i rigs su byung | |mkha' 'gro ma yin dpa' bo'i gces|
yā navāmbudavarṇābhā kiṃcitpāṇḍarasaṃ{n}nibhā | lalāṭe bibhratī paṭṭaṃ gṛhe cakraṃ ca yatnataḥ |
samayādyaiḥ pūjayantī gāyantī nṛtyantī sadā | vārāhīkulajā jñeyā ḍākinī sā mahābalā |
%|sprin sngon dag dang 'dra ba ni| |rtag tu dpal bar thod yod pa| |khyim du 'khor lo 'di byed cing | |rtag tu de la mchod byed pa| |rdo rje phag mo'i rigs 'byung ba'i| |mkha' 'gro ma ni stobs kyis dregs|
yā kṛṣṇāñjanavarṇābhā danturā raudramānasām | sadā śvāna[=snāna]ratā śuddhā prakṛtyā mandabhāṣiṇī |
bhūmau li[\ms27\verso]khitvā kuliśaṃ pūjayen niśārdhake | nairā<t>myākulajā jñeyā sākṣāt sā vajraḍākinī |
%|mig sman nam mkha' sngon po'i mdog |so ni mtho ba drag mo dang | |rtag tu khrus la dga' ba dang | |mang du smra bar mi byed cing | |khyim du rdo rje 'dri ba dang | |mtshan mo byed na mchod par byed| |rdo rje phag mo'i rigs 'byung ba'i| |dpal ldan rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma|
taptakāñcanavarṇābhā nārī atilomasā | mallikotpalagandhāṅgī subhagā satyavādinī |
lalāṭe kuliśaṃ sākṣād gṛhe cāpi tathaiva ca | likhitvā pūjayen nityam atidarpākulā hi sā |

āsāṃ viśuddhirūpeṇa nāḍyoktā 'dvayayogataḥ |
etayo yat sukhaṃ cāsti tad ahaṃ paramārthataḥ ||
yasmāt sukhamayaṃ sarvam avidyāvāsanānvitam |

%|'jums pa dang ni 'byed pa yis| |rlung gis me ni khong du chud|

%|rnam par rtog pa'i phung po'i gzugs| |thams cad lus ni sreg pa'o| |de yi me 'bar yang dag gis| |bde chen 'khor lo ba zhu bar 'gyur| |bzhu pas rtsa ni thams cad las| |mchog gi bdud rtsi 'jag pa'o| |yang ni lus ni sos 'gyur te| |lha yi sku ni thob par 'gyur| |de ni go mas pa'i sbyor ba yis| |rtse gcig gis ni goms par bya|
nopayuktā'thavā kiṃcid darśanāyopayujyate |
na tathā sarvakālaṃ ca prādhānye parikalpite ||
%|phyi yi phyag rgya med na yang | |bde ba 'byung bar 'gyur ba yod| | blta ba tsam ni dgos pa ste| |de bzhin thams cad thams cad min|
yasmād vikalpo janayet paścāt siddhir bhaven na ca |
yathāndhakāre vastv ekaṃ dīpa[¯]bhāve na dṛśyate |
dīpadarśanatatprāptyā paścād dīpaṃ parityajet |
yasmāt prayojanaṃ tena na tu dīpena sarvadā ||
%|phyi nasarnam par rtog pa skye| |de la dngos grub med par 'gyur| |ji ltar mun pa'i snod rnams ni| |mar me med na mthong mi 'gyur| |mar mes mthong bar gyur tsam na| |phyi nas mar me dor bar bya| |snod na 'bad de bsrung bya se| |mar mes don bya mi dgos so|
tasmād evaṃvidhānena nāḍīrūpasvarūpikā |
jñātavyā bhāvakiḥ samyak dīpaprajñāntakaṃ khalu ||
%|mar me'i mtshan nyid 'di ru rtsa| |rtsa ni shes rab mtshan nyid do|
atha vā kathitopāyaḥ saukhyamuktipra[\ms32\verso]darśakaḥ |
darśakaṃ vāhave jñānam utpattikramapakṣakaḥ ||
prajñātaḥ prāpyate cādau yat sukhaṃ bhavate <tu> tat |
prajñājñānābhiṣekāgryam utpattikramapakṣakaḥ ||
%|thabs ni bad bzhes su bstan| |bde ba ye shes zhes byar dran| |bde ba'i ye shes rang bzhin ni| |gang zhig shes rab ma las rnyed| |da ni shes rab ye shes dbang | |de ni rdzogs pa'i rim pa'o|
tad eva mahāsukhajñānaṃ maṇḍate[=le] saṃvyavasthitam |
mahāsukhaṃ ca dvividhaṃ sāṃvṛtaṃ paramārthikam ||
%|de dngos bde chen ye shes ni| |dkyil 'khor nang du yang dag bzhugs|
%|bde ba chen po gnyis su gyis| |kun rdzob dang ni don dam mo|
ānandādiprabhedena jñātavyaṃ bodhidāyakam |
yasmād antaḥsthitaṃ sākṣād ānandānāṃ paraṃ sukham ||
tasmāt kramadvayaṃ teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu cādarāt |

aloka spandanaṃ saṅgo mucchanaṃ cānu<|kra|>tatkramāt |
catuṣṭayam idaṃ jñeyaṃ hetumūlātmakaṃ śubham |
ādāv evobhayor dṛṣṭe mīlanād rāgasambhavaḥ ||
tasmād ānandakodbhūto vicitrakṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ |
%|snang ba reg bya sbyor ba dang | |'gro bde ni bzhi pa'o| |rim pa yis ni shes par bya| |bskyed pa'i rim pa'i phyogs kyi ni| |shes rab ma yi lta ba yis| |gnyis ka'i 'dod chags 'byung bar 'gyur| |de tshe dga' ba' 'byung ba ste| |rnam pa sna tshogs skad cig ldan|
yadā prajñāsahasparśalakṣaṇa ekaṃ sādara[\ms33\recto]m bhavet ||
tadātra paramānando jāyate śuddhalakṣaṇaḥ |

sa cātra viramānandaḥ prodbhūta prajvālite 'nale ||

bhaṭṭārikā prāha ||
yaś caturvidhopāyenānandādīnāṃ kvacit saṃkṣepaṇārthaḥ sūcitam ihāpi tac ca vistareṇa kathaya<|si|><sva> bhagavan mayi ||
bhagavān āha ||
prādhānyaina prajñāvyāpāras tu ānandādīnām utpattiḥ |
prajñāvalokanāt kāmaḥ | kāmād dharṣobhayor bhavet |
sparśāt saiva punaḥ pūrṇaiḥ pūrṇānandaṃ karoti saḥ ||
%|nu mas reg pas blta ba ni| |'dod pa de ni dang por dga'| |phyi nas de dngos rgyas pa ni| |gang tshe nges par yin par 'gyur| |de tshe skad cig gis de rgyas| |mchog gis dga' ba byed pa'o|

yadā punaḥ saiva mahārāgena jvalate [\ms33\verso] |
tadā spharati jvālābinduś ca viramānandalakṣaṇaḥ |
%|gang tshe de nyid shin tu yis| |'dod chags me ni 'bar 'gyur na| |de tshe 'bar ba'i thig le ni| |dga' dral dga' ba 'bab pa'o|
vajrī cābjī ca ramate viramānande 'pi sādarāt ||
jñeyam ṛ[=u]tpattim apy evaṃ o<> (?) ājñānena varjitā |
%|rdo rje padma rol byed pa| |de bzhin dga' bral dga' ba'o| |de ni bskyed pa'i rim pa yin| |u ta'i ye shes dang bral lo|
punar eva yadā bhāvagrāmye vismṛtike sati ||
oṃ (?) āsā<'>naramadhye tu majjamānāpadātmakam |
%|yang ni gang tshe u ta ni| |skad cig nyid las gcig tu 'gro| |de tshe u ta'i thig le ni| |gsum gyi rnam par dag par 'gro|
tadā 'nusmarate bhrānta oṃ ā binduṃ sukhātmakam ||
evaṃ bhagavatī prajñā utpannakramapakṣataḥ |
%|de nas der ni lhan cig skyes| |rdo rje lta bu'i bde ba byed| |de ni bcom ldan shes rab ma| | rdzogs pa'i rim pa'i phyogs yin no|
tanoti sahajānandaṃ yatra vajropamaṃ kṣaṇāt ||
tatrāhaṃ vyāpakaḥ samyak nānyo stri surat<|i|>eśvari |
%|u ta'i sgras ni bde bar bstan| |legs dgyes dbang mo de ni bdag
punar asyaiva vakṣyāmi caturyogaṃ samāsataḥ |
kāmānandaś ca kampaś ca drutaś cānandakas tathā ||
%|de ru sbyor ba bzhi rnams ni| |rgyas par bdag gis bshad par bya| |'dod dang 'dar dang 'gro ba dang | |dga' ba dang ni bzhi yin te|
ity eṣa prathamo yogaḥ sa<|ṅgā|><jñā>naṃ tasya nirṇayam |
pūrṇānandas tathā sthairya [\ms34\recto] <ta>syodbhavaḥ paramas tathā ||
%|de ni dang po'i sbyor ba yin| |de ni dga' ba'i don yin no| |rgyas dang 'dun dang 'byung ba dang | |mchog gi dga' ba bzhi yin te|
ity evaṃ paścime dvitīyayogaḥ paramānandasya nirṇayam |
jvālānandaś ca binduś ca ghūrmākhyo viramas tathā ||
%|de ni sbyor ba gnyis pa yin| |mchog gi dga' ba'i don yin no| |'bar dang thig le myos pa dang | |dga' bral dang ni bzhi yin te|
ity evaṃ tṛtīyo yogo viramānandasya nirṇayam |
oṃ ānandaś ca nādaś ca praśāntaḥ sahajas tathā ||
%|de ni sbyor ba gsum pa yin| dga' bral "80b" dga' ba'i don yin no| |u ta dang ni tshul dang gnyid| |lhan cig skyes dga' bzhi pa'o|
iti yogaś caturthoktaḥ sahajākhyasya nirṇayam |
ṣoḍaśānandabhedena rājaty eva catuṣṭayam |
%|de ni sbyor ba bzhi ba yin| |dga' ba'i bya brag bcu drug go| |bzhi rnams gzhan min 'di ru ni| |rab kyi don ni 'di ltar 'gyur|
tasmād bindukrameṇāsya mukhyam athaṃ śranad dhruvam |
kāmānandaḥ sa vikhyāto yataḥ prajñājanito rasaḥ |
%|'dod pa 'dun pa yin par bshad| |dpal ldan shes rab mas sbul lo|
tasmāt svakīye 'smin manaḥ kṣubdhaṃ dṛṣṭvā na muñcayet |
yadā bhagavatī prajñā mahārāgeṇa pūrṇatām |
prāptā svapi<vi>(?)sate yasya pūraṇāt paramaṃ tadā ||
tasmād evaṃ yadā samya[\ms34\verso]k sa bāhyābhyantaraś ciram |
jvalate paramānando mastakād drāvayet tadā ||
jvālāvibhur bhavet sṛṣṭaṃ viramānandalakṣaṇam |
punar eva yadā devi svasaukhyakulamānasā ||
saṃpadyate tadā samyak oṃākṛṣṭiṃ k{r}aroti saḥ |
tayā virājate turyaṃ sahajānandalakṣaṇam ||
%|de ni 'dun pa las ni dkrug |rang gi yid kyis byed pa'o| |'dod pa'i sgras ni dga' ba yin| |mthong ba las ni byed pa'o| |de nyid bcom ldan shes rab ma| |'dod chags chen pos rgyas pa na| |de tshe rgyas pa yin par 'gyur| |mchog gi dga' ba gnyis pa'o| |rgyas pa'i dus su phyi dang nang | |gang tshe shin tu 'bar 'gyur na| |de tshe yan la ga ra pa de ni| |skad cig gis ni zhu bar 'gyur| |da ni 'bar ba'i thig les ni| |ming ni gsal bar gyur pa'o| |de ni dga' bral dga' ba ste| |gsum pa zhes bya bshad pa'o| |yang na de nyid gtso mo ni| |mchog gi rnam pa rab bde ba| |de tshe u ta'i dgug pa ni| |srid pa dang bral byed pa'o| |de ni bzhi ba yang dag mchog |lhan cig skyes dga' bstan pa'o|
sā coākṛṣtibhir bindur mukhyaṃ cānte vatiṣṭhate |
bindv ānandādibhedena sahaja eva nāparam ||
yasmāc cittasvarūpākhyaṃ tasmāt sarvatra saṃsthitam |
mahāsukhacakroṣṇīśād yāvad bhru ca kroṭaraṃ ||
tāvad ānandarūpasya bindvāsya sadāspadam |
bhrūcakrāt kaṇṭhacakrāntaṃ tāvat paramasya ca ||
kaṇṭhāntād dhṛdayaṃ yāvad viramākhyāsya saṃsthitaḥ |
tāvad anenaiva punar dvi[\ms35\recto]tīyaṃ cocyate kramam ||
nābhicakrād yāvac cāntas trivalī tāvad āspadaṃ |
sa bindvānandarasya cotpannakramapakṣataḥ ||
trivalīsthānād guhyamūlāṅ granthibhāgasya coktivat |
paramānandaṃ sya vijñeyo viramasya ca kathyate ||
granthisthānān maṇimukhaṃ viramasyāspadaṃ smṛtam |
parameṇa sahāsyaiva hṛdayaṃ sahajasya ca ||   ||
%|de ni u ta'i dgug pa ni| |thig le'i thar pa gsum gyi mthar| |lhan skyes kyi ni gnas su'o| |dga' ba la sogs bye brag gis| |lhan cig skyes rim shes par bya| |bde ba chen po'i gtsug tor nas| |smin phrag 'khor lo'i dbus yan chad| |de tsam dga' ba'i gnas yin te| |thig le gcig bu'i mtshan nyid do| |smin phrag 'khor los mgrin 'khor lo| |de tsam mchog gi dga' ba'o| |mgrin pa'i gnas nas snying 'khor lo| |de tsam dga' bral dga' ba'o| |de nas yang ni rim pa yis| |rim pa'i gnas ni rab tu bshad| |lte ba'i 'khor nas rgyu zhabs su| |de tsam du ni dga' ba yin| |rgyu zhabs su ni mdud pa'i rtsar| |de tsam mchog gi dga' ba'o| |mdud pa'i gnas nas rin chen mkhar| |de tsam dga' bral dga' ba'o| |rdo rje rin chen rtse nang yod| |lhan skyes de ni don dam pa'o|

bhagavan jñānaḍākeśa saṃśayaṃ kiṃcid asti me |

%|bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa|
nādaṃ [\ms35\verso] lalāṭasthānasthaṃ kaṇṭhe śu[¯]nyaṃ vyavasthitam |

%|chul ni dpral ba'i gnas su ste| |thig le mgrin pa'i gnas su bzhugs| |char yang snying ga'i gnas su shes| |ye shes lte ba'i gnas su bzhugs|

tatra teṣāṃ bhave jñānaṃ yeṣāṃ nābhipade sthitiḥ ||
%|rtog pa las ni rtog pa med| |gang du phyin nas 'gro ba na| |de ni de rnams rang bzhin gnas| |lte ba'i gnas su rab tu grags|
yadāmṛtaṃ yāti jñānaṃ teṣāṃ tadāspadaṃ sadā |
amṛtaṃ bodhicittānāṃ svarūpaṃ śu[¯]nyatādiṣu ||

viramākhyaṃ tu hṛdaye nā<|ḍau|><bhau> sahajalakṣaṇam |
%|snying ga ru ni dga' bral te| |lte ba ru ni lhan cig syes|
evamādiṣu saṃsthānaṃ trikoṇaṃ tasya mūrdhni hi ||

%|rtsa yi kun spyod sbyor ba yis| |dga' ba la sogs rnyed par 'gyur| |dpral ba'i steng na padma yod| |de yi steng na gtsug tor ro| |de ni gsang chen ye shes te| |rgyal ba yis kyang mi shes so| |lte ba'i 'og gi gsum gnas su| |de yi steng du rgyu zhabs so| |gsum dngos 'og gi lte ba'i dbyibs| |de ru rdo rje byad gzugs gsal| |gsum dngos gnas na rtsa rnams kyi| |yang dag mchog gi gnas yin no|

%|bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa|
atiguhyataraṃ praśnaṃ na kaścit pṛcchyate bhṛśam |

%|gang tshe legs dgyas shin tu bde| |shes rab thabs gyi sbyor ba las| |de tshe rtog pa med pa'i lam| |byang chub sems ni 'byung bar 'gyur|
tasmin yadā prāṇavāyur na tyaje<t> yoginā tadā |

tataḥ prāṇopari manaḥ pratikūlena tad vahet |

tadātra gacchate nādha yathā niśchidrabhājane{ḥ} ||

%|de yi gnas su dga' ba bzhi| |ro gnyis dang ni ldan pa'i| |mgrin pa nas ni snying gar gnas| |yang ni de tsam bzhi yin no| |mchog gi dga' ba'i bzhi yin te| |ro gnyis dang ni ldan pa'o| |snying ga'i gnas nas rin chen sker| |yang ni de tsam bzhi yin no| | dga' bral dga' ba'i bzhi yin te| |ro gnyis dang ni ldan pa'o| |rin chen ske nas steng gi gnas| |yang ni de tsam bzhi yin no| |lhan cig skyes dga' bzhi yin te| |ro gnyis dang ni ldan pa'o| |dga' ba bcu drug thig le ste| |zla ba'i cha ru rab tu dran|
ālirūpās tu te rūpā jñeyāś cakreṣv anukramāt |
yathoktās tu tathāpy atra sūryāsya dvādaśā smṛtāḥ |
dvādaśārdhānyarūpeṇānandemadānandās tathā brahmavihārāś ca [\ms39\verso] tathā rasāḥ |
brahmānaneṣu rahitā kalāś candrārkayoḥ smṛtāḥ |

viramākhye trayas tv anye sākāre kathitās tathā ||
sahajānas tribhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ varjitā 'hy evamādibhiḥ |
%|A li thig ler shes par "82b" bya| |'khor lo bzhi yi rim pas so| |tshangs pa yi ni zhal dang bral| |ro dang bcas pa bdag gi zhal| |bcu gnyis de ni nyi zhes shes| |kA li thig ler rab tu grags| |bcu gnyis phyed ni bde thig le| |dga' dang mchog gi dga' bar dran| |dga' bral dga' ba'i thig le gsum| |rang bzhin gsum drang bral ba'o| |yang ni thams cad bdag nyid nyid|
atha sarvātmakaḥ saikaḥ sarvatra kathito mayā ||
saikaś caturvidho ktoyaḥ sa hetuphalasaṃyutaḥ |

nirmāṇa{ṃ}dharmasambhogamahāsukham iti kramāt |

ālijñānaṃ bhavec cittaṃ kāliś caitasikaṃ smṛtam |

%|char yang sbyor ba nang du min| |nang du de ni rtog med ma| |mdud pa'i nang du g.yas dang g.yon| |ro ma dang ni brkyang ma gnas|
granthibhyām antaraikaikaṃ yogaṃ vai vāvadhāritam \dandab
yatronyatrasthitā nāḍyas tasmāt pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ \veg
dvādaśāṅulaparyantaṃ sūtraṃ nigra<nthi>lakṣaṇam \dandab
mahāsukhābjahṛdayād yāvat kaṇṭhakaroṭakam \veg
ataḥ paraṃ granthipramuktaḥ paryantasya vacanasya ca \dandab
kaṇṭhapṛṣṭhād yāvat cchānta tāvad yogacatuṣṭayam \veg
antaḥsthānād dhṛtpradeśaṃ tāvat proktaṃ dvitīyakam \dandab
hṛtpradeśān nābhimū<|ṇḍa|>laṃ tāvat proktaṃ tṛtīyakam \veg
nābhisthānād ratnakaṇṭhaṃ tāvad uktaṃ caturthakam \dandab
evaṃ caturvidhaṃ yogaṃ kalpanandobhayor yutam \veg
%|mdud pa gnyis dbus gcig gcig ste| |sum cu gnyis kyisabacu drug dran| |bde chen 'khor lo mgrin pa gnas| |lte ba'i steng ni yan chad do| |sor bcu gnyis kyi skud pa ni| | "89a" mdud pa med pa gcig du'o| |de 'og ma dud pa dang ldan pa| |rlung gi 'khor lo yan chad do| |spyi bo nas ni mgrin yan chad| |de tsam sbyor ba bzhi yin no| |mgrin pa'i 'og nas snying ga'i gnas| |de tsam gzhan ni bzhi yin no| |snying ga'i gnas nas lte ba'i bar| |de tsam gzhan yang bzhi yin no| |lte ba nas ni nor bu'i mgrin| |de tsam gzhan ni bzhi yin no| |de ni sbyor ba bzhi ru bshad| |char yang dga' ba dang bral ba|
kāmo vajratpaṇakaṃ pa plavanaś ca su[\ms54\recto]dhānvitaḥ \dandab
ānando <'>bdhisamāyuktaḥ proktam ādyacatuṣṭayam \veg
%|'dod dang rdul dang 'dar dang stobs| |'gro dang bdud rtsi yang yin te| |dga' dang padma de bzhin te| |de ni dang po'i sbyor ba bzhi|
utsaṃ<ddā>khyāmumusnānādomodyācāpi praśāntakaḥ \dandab
tvaṃjatyā saha saṃyuktaś caturthaṃ tu catuṣṭayam \veg
%|rgyas dang rdul dang mdud dang myos| |'byung ba dang ni mtshan mo dang | |mchog gi dga' dang ro yin te| |de ni gnyis pa'i sbyor ba'o| |'bar dang zhi dang thig le gnas| |myos dang 'og ma de bzhin no| |dga' bral dga'ang 'gug pa ste| |de ni gsum pa'i sbyor ba'o| |u ta'ang ba ma tshul rlung ma| |gnyid dang me ma de bzhin no| |lhan cig skyes dga' ma skyes pa| |de ni sbyor ba bzhi pa'o|
ratnakaṇṭhaprāptamātre pūrṇendur bhavate sphuṭam \dandab
ubhayoḥ saiva bhijñeyā kalānandair alaṅkṛtaḥ \veg
%|rin chen sker phyin tsam na| |de ni zla ba gang bar dran| |dga' ba yod pa'i sbyor ba ni| |de ni shar bar shes par bya|
jātiṣañcadaśānāṃ ca ṣoḍaśī naiva kalpyate \dandab
udayavyayahīnatvād yogas tasyā na vidyate \veg
%|bcwa lnga pa ni 'byung ba'i rgyu| |bcu drug ba la rtag mi byed| |skye dang 'jig pa med pa'i phyir| |de la sbyor ba med pa'o|
kalānāṃ tu yathā yogam asti devi tathā śṛṇu \dandab
ṣoḍaśasya tathā'rkasya dvayor yatra samāgamam \veg
%|char yang rnam skyed sbyor ba ni| |rtse gcig gis ni khyod nyon cig |bcu drug de bzhi na nyi ma dang | |gang du gnyig sbyor bar 'gyur|
tatrotpattipadaṃ jñeyaṃ niṣkalaṃ sahajātmakam \dandab
tat pitā sarvabhūtānāṃ prajñopāyasvarūpakam \veg
%|de la ma skyes cha zhes bya| |de rang ngo bo rang bzhin no| |bcu drug de bzhin nyi ma dang | |char yang 'gyur ba'i rgyun yin no| |de ni 'byung ba kun gyi pha| |thabs dang shes rab mnyam par ldan|
ṣoḍaśāsyaṃ bhavet prajñā upāyaḥ kathito raviḥ \dandab
uddānandaḥ samākhyāto vira[\ms54\verso]maḥ paramātmakaḥ \veg
%|bcu drug pa ni shes rab yin| |nyi ma thabs su bstan pa'o| | bcu drug phan chad shes par bya| |nyi ma mchog tu dran pa'o|
etat tayor antarasthaṃ <|bhavet prajñā | upā|> yad āvācyaṃ sahajaṃ ca tat \dandab
etat prayojanaṃ devi kālasya kathitaṃ mayā \veg
%|de gnyis dbus bzhugs rang bzhin ni| |ngag gi lam gyis rnyed par dka'| |de lta bu yi dus kyi don| |yang dag par ni bdag gis bshad|
ataḥ paraṃ nāsti guhyaṃ trilokeṣu durlabham \dandab
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena na dātavyaṃ tu kasyacit \veg % ||   ||
%|'di las gzhan mchog gsang pa ni| |zhal "89b" bzang yang dag med pa'o| |'bad pa yis ni sba bar bya| |sku la yang ni mi sbyin no|
iti śrīmahāmudrātilake kālaprayojanasahajārthapaṭalaḥ ṣaṭviṃśatitamaḥ \ujvers\veg % ||   ||

%|dpal phyag rgya chen po'i thig le las dus kyi don gyi rang bzhin gyi don zhes bya ba'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa drug pa'o|| ||

\vers % 28.13
sukhamadhyān mahāmudrā labhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \dandab
\vvar{\va °madhyān] \stand, °madhyāt \ms}
\barmi{\va mahām[\ms56\recto]drā}
śūnyatāmadhyasūtrasya prāntaḥsthāno madoditā \veg
\vvar{\vc °sūtrasya] \emend, °śutrasya \ms}
\versvege
%|bde ba'i nang nas phyag rgya che| |the tshom med par rnyed par 'gyur|
atisukṣmyaṃ tarākārā jñāninām apy agocarā
mūlasthānān mūlapadaṃ tāvad vyāpya vyavasthitā
yat tvayā evan tu sā mahāmudrāsvarūpikā
sattejaḥ sarvabhāvānāṃ prajñārūpeṇa saṃsthitāḥ
bāhye cābhyantare caiva sahajānandadāyakaḥ
%|char yang dbus na rtsa yi gnas| |phra mo'i phra mo yang dag mchog |rtsa yi gnas na rtsa yi gnas| |de tsam la ni nges khyab bo| |phyag rgya chen po'i rang bzhin ni| |lha mo chen mo de ni khyod| |nga yi 'od ni yang dag khyod| |phyi dang nang ni yod pa'o|
yo na vetti mahāmudrāṃ so buddhāpi na vajradhṛk
yo pi cedaṃ mahāmudrātilakaṃ naiva prabudhayte
so pi jñānaphalaṃ samyak na prāpnoti kadācana
yoginīyogayos tantraṃ lakṣam ekaṃ na vindate
tathāpy anenaiva vinā buddhatvaṃ prāpyate na hi
%|gang zhig phyag rgya che mi shes| |sangs rgyas yin yang rnal 'byor min| |phyag rgya chen po'i thig le 'di| |gang zhig dri med shes pa min| |rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma yi rgyud| |'bum gcig rnal 'byor nges shes kyang | |de lta mong kyi 'bras rab nges| |nam yang 'byung bar mi 'gyur ro|
sarvadharmaparijñānād buddho bhavati niścitam
mahāmudrāparijñānā[\ms56\verso]c chrīmān vajradharo bhavet
etat sahajajñānaṃ hi mahāmudrāsvarūpakam
%|thams cad chos kun rab shes na| |de ni nges par sangs rgyas yin| |phyag rgya chen po rab shes na| |de ni dpal rdor sems dpar 'gyur| |de yi rang bzhin ye shes ni| |phyag rgya chen po'i mtshan nyid do|
evaṃ śrutvā dvāsaptatisahasraḍākinībhiḥ saha bhaṭṭārikā bhagavataḥ pādau śirasābhivandya tripradakṣiṇīkṛtya tvoṣṭaṃ kṣatajaṃ nivedayanti sma
%|de skad ces thos nas bcom ldan 'das ma bye ba 'bum phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis kyi mkha' 'gro ma dang bcas pas bskor ba lan gsum byas nas phyag 'tshal te khrag dron mos mchod do|
tataḥ sarvaḍākinyo mahāmūdrātilakatantrarājaṃ śrutvā mūkībhāvena vyavasthitāḥ
%|bcom ldan 'das la mkha' 'gro ma thams cad kyis phyag rgya chen po thig le'i rgyud thos nas dga' mgu yi rangs te cang mi smra bar zhugs so|
punar api gāḍham āliṅganaṃ kṛtvā bhaṭṭārikā idum ūvāca
%|yang dam du 'khyud pa byas nas 'di skad ces "90b" gsol to|
maṇḍalasya vidhis tāvat mūlatantra prakāśitaḥ
tatra me saṃśayaṃ bhagavan <tvaṃ brūhi> yoginīprabho
parapīṭhaṃ cātmapīṭhaṃ na śrutaṃ prāk kvacit mayā
%|bcom ldan rnal 'byor ma yi bdag |cung zad bdag la the tshom skyes| |gang zhig dkyil 'khor cho ga ni| |rtsa ba yi ni rgyud du bshad| |bdag dang gzhan gyi gnas de ni| |sngar ni bdag gis ma shes so|
bhagavān āha
%|bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa|
śṛṇu samyak pravakṣyāmy ātmapīṭhaṃ paraṃ tathā
parapīṭhaṃ bhagaṃ proktaṃ ātmapīṭhaṃ tu [\ms57\recto] karṇikā
%|bdag gi gnas dngagazhan de bzhin| |nyon cig yang dag bdag gis bshad| |gzhan gyi gnas ni bha gar brjod| |bdag gi gnas ni snying po'o|
parapīṭhaṃ dvārabāhyaṃ ātmapīṭhaṃ tu maṇḍalam
parapīṭhaṃ vāyur yogaś cātmapīṭhaṃ tu yoginī
%|sgo yi phyi rol gzhan gyi gnas| |bdag gis gnas ni dkyil 'khor ro| |yang na gzhan gnas sbyor ba yin| |nye ba'i sbyor ba bdag gnas so|
yogaḥ svarga iṣṭi prokto yogo martyas tathaiva ca
upayogaṃ tu pātālaṃ merumūrdhni tathā paraḥ
dharmā'bjaṃ hṛdayaḥ svargā martyaś cāsyaiva karṇikā
%|sbyor ba mtho ris zhes su bstan| |de bzhin du yang mi yul sbyor| |nye ba'i sbyor ba sa 'og ste| |mtho ris chos kyi snying padma| |nye ba'i sbyor ba ri rab rtse| |mi yul lte ba'i lte ba'o| | sa 'og sprul pa'i snying po ste| |ri rab rtse mo gsang sna'o|
e<ta>t paraṃ mahāguhyaṃ noktaṃ samyaṅ mayā kvacit
rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnena dātavyaṃ tu <na> kasyacit
%|de ltar gzhan ni gsang chen po| |yang dag gzhan du ngas ma bshad| |'bad de de ni bsrung bya ste| |su la yang ni mi sbyin no|
yoginītantrarājedaṃ yogināṃ saṃprakāśayet
atha vā bodhisattvasya kṛtvā samyak parīkṣaṇam % ||   ||
%|rnal 'byor ma rgyud dbang phyug rgyal| |rnal 'byor ma la bshad par bya| | yang na sems dpa' chen po 'di| |legs par brtags nas gsal bar bya|
iti śrīmahāmudrātilake ātmapīṭhādinirṇayo nāmāṣṭāviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ || 28 ||

%|dpal he ru ka chen po'i dang po'i dang po grub pa| de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang bla las kyang ches gsang ba'i rgyud phyi ma dpal phyag rgya chen po'i thig le rnal 'byor ma chen po'i rgyud kyi rgyal po'i mnga' bdag |thams cad mkhyen pas kyang rnyed par dka' ba| rmad du byung ba'i rmad du byung ba zhes bya ba'i le'u ste| nyi shu rtsa brgyad pa'o|| ||
śrīmahāmudrātilakatantrarā[\ms57\verso]jādhirājam ādisiddhaṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ guhyād guhyataraṃ sarvajñajñānagocaraṃ paramamahādbhutaṃ samāptam ||   ||
%dpal phyag rgya chen po'i thig le rnal 'byor ma chen mo'i rgyud kyi rgyal po'i mnga' bdag ces bya ba rdzogs so|| ||
[2] || ye dharmmā hetuprabhavā , hetu teṣāñ ca tathāgato , hy avadat teṣāñ ca yo nirodha evaṃvādī mahāśramaṇaḥ || śubhaṃ || [3] || svasti śrīsaṃvat sāgarayugadurggatiphārggu<ṇa3>māse śucipakṣe , ṣoḍaśakalānvitaparvvadine , saṃpūrṇṇam abhūt || [4] @ śrītalu<˜>mulamahāvihāre vāsthita , śrīvajrodbhavācāryyarājamānasiṃhena svārthe bodhanārthaṃ bhavacakrottārarnanimittena sva[5]yaṃ mahāyānatantram alikhad iti ||   || abdau yuga netra <324> bhuvanam iti thuti saṃvatsara ḅaṅ gu pothi sasvakhyaṃ hleyā julo śubhaṃ || / 947 ḅaṅ ,,
% thuti - this/that
% gu - what, who, which
% hleyā - to move, transfer; julo - practically past tense
% 947 = 1827; 324 = 1204
% Sikhamu bāhā, see Locke 1985:258-263
% rgya gar gyi slob dpon rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po dpal shes rab gsang ba'i zhal sngar| ka ma chos kyi ye shes dang | phyug mtshams dgra bcom gyis dad cing gus pas zhus te bsgyur cing gtan la phab pa|| ||
